## Common questions

### Where can I find AI Research Scientist / Research Engineer jobs?

This list has 300 open AI Research Scientist / Research Engineer roles from 185 companies, refreshed every 2 days from landed's live job database. Each role links to a landed job page with the employer's own application.

### Which companies are hiring for AI Research Scientist / Research Engineer roles right now?

Top-rated companies hiring now include Liquid AI, Lila Sciences, Hark, EliseAI, Generalist, Horizon3.ai, Figma and Nominal. 24 roles are at YC-backed companies.

### Are there remote AI Research Scientist / Research Engineer jobs?

Yes. 79 of the 300 roles are remote, marked 🌐 in the list.

### Are there entry-level AI Research Scientist / Research Engineer jobs?

Yes. 67 roles are internships or entry level.

### How can I apply to more of these, faster?

[landed](https://app.landed.jobs/signup?from=github&cta=github-list-bulk&item=applied-scientist-and-research-engineer-jobs&utm_source=github&utm_medium=referral&utm_campaign=github-applied-scientist-and-research-engineer-jobs&utm_content=faq) matches the roles here to your resume and applies for you, and Enzo drafts your answers for you to review.

---

<details>
<summary><b>How this list is built</b></summary>

This list is generated from landed's live job database and refreshed every two days. It keeps open roles posted in the last 90 days that link to an employer application. US-based and worldwide-remote roles come first, then the rest of the world. Within each group, roles from top-rated companies (by TrueUp lists, Y Combinator, valuation, funding stage and investors) come first among roles posted in the last 30 days, then newer roles. Each company appears at most 5 times, and staffing agencies are left out. Company badges come from TrueUp and Y Combinator data.

Spotted a closed or wrong role? [Open an issue](https://github.com/landedjobs/applied-scientist-and-research-engineer-jobs/issues/new).

</details>
